# 金融账户


## 账户交易明细-分页


**接口地址**:`/zhisuancf/manage/api/v1/financialAccounts/{accountId}/transDetail/page`


**请求方式**:`POST`


**请求数据类型**:`application/x-www-form-urlencoded,application/json`


**响应数据类型**:`*/*`


**接口描述**:<p>账户交易明细-分页</p>

**请求参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 请求类型    | 是否必须 | 数据类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | -------- | -------- | ------ |
|accountId|账户id|path|true|integer(int64)||
|x-auth-token|token 值|header|true|||
|pageWrapperTransactionDetailEntity|PageWrapperTransactionDetailEntity|body|true|PageWrapperTransactionDetailEntity|PageWrapperTransactionDetailEntity|
|&emsp;&emsp;success|||false|boolean||

|&emsp;&emsp;rows|||false|array|TransactionDetailEntity|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_remark|备注||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_created_by|创建人||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_creation_date|创建时间||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_by|最后更新人||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_date|最后更新时间||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_enabled_flag|有效性||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;id|Id||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;workspaceId|工作空间id||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;accountId|账户id||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;manageCompanyId|管理机构(1.【应用】管理该应用下所有公司和个人的账户2.【机构】管理会员账户)3.没有时填工作空间管理方公司id||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;name|变动名称||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;inOutType|出入账类型,可用值:OTHER,CHARGE,REFUND,PURCHASE,OUT,IN,PROFITSHARING_TALLY,PROFITSHARING_THIRD,PROFITSHARING_TO_ACCOUNT,INCOME_PROMOTION,INCOME_COOPERATION,INCOME_ASSET,INCOME_OPERATION,INCOME_IOT,REFUND_TRANSFER_TO_THIRD,REFUND_TO_ACCOUNT,REFUND_INCOME_TO_THIRD,WITHDRAW_TO_THIRD,ACCOUNT_WITHDRAW,PRESENT,PACKAGE,CPC,CPCHARGE||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;changeType|变动流向类型,可用值:OTHER,CHARGE,REFUND,PURCHASE,OUT,IN,PROFITSHARING_TALLY,PROFITSHARING_THIRD,PROFITSHARING_TO_ACCOUNT,INCOME_PROMOTION,INCOME_COOPERATION,INCOME_ASSET,INCOME_OPERATION,INCOME_IOT,REFUND_TRANSFER_TO_THIRD,REFUND_TO_ACCOUNT,REFUND_INCOME_TO_THIRD,WITHDRAW_TO_THIRD,ACCOUNT_WITHDRAW,PRESENT,PACKAGE,CPC,CPCHARGE||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;changeSubType|变动子类型,可用值:OTHER,CHARGE,REFUND,PURCHASE,OUT,IN,PROFITSHARING_TALLY,PROFITSHARING_THIRD,PROFITSHARING_TO_ACCOUNT,INCOME_PROMOTION,INCOME_COOPERATION,INCOME_ASSET,INCOME_OPERATION,INCOME_IOT,REFUND_TRANSFER_TO_THIRD,REFUND_TO_ACCOUNT,REFUND_INCOME_TO_THIRD,WITHDRAW_TO_THIRD,ACCOUNT_WITHDRAW,PRESENT,PACKAGE,CPC,CPCHARGE||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;amount|动账数额如：金额、流量||false|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;balance|变动后账户余额||false|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;nonWithdrawBalance|变动后不可提现余额(如：赠送，积分兑换的都在这里)||false|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;orderId|关联的订单号id||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;orderNo|关联的订单号||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;payStatus|付款状态,可用值:UNPAID,PAYING,PARTIALPAID,PAID,PAIDCANCEL,PAYFAIL,DISABLE||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;payTime|付款时间||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;payChannel|付款渠道,可用值:WECHAT_JSAPI,WECHAT_NATIVE,WECHAT_APP,WECHAT_MICROPAY,WECHAT_MWEB,WECHAT_FACEPAY,WALLET_VIRTUAL_NW,WALLET_VIRTUAL,BANK||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;billPeriod|所属账期如26010515||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;periodEndDate|所属账期截止时间||false|string||

|&emsp;&emsp;name|变动名称|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;inOutType|出入账类型,可用值:OTHER,CHARGE,REFUND,PURCHASE,OUT,IN,PROFITSHARING_TALLY,PROFITSHARING_THIRD,PROFITSHARING_TO_ACCOUNT,INCOME_PROMOTION,INCOME_COOPERATION,INCOME_ASSET,INCOME_OPERATION,INCOME_IOT,REFUND_TRANSFER_TO_THIRD,REFUND_TO_ACCOUNT,REFUND_INCOME_TO_THIRD,WITHDRAW_TO_THIRD,ACCOUNT_WITHDRAW,PRESENT,PACKAGE,CPC,CPCHARGE|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;changeType|变动流向类型,可用值:OTHER,CHARGE,REFUND,PURCHASE,OUT,IN,PROFITSHARING_TALLY,PROFITSHARING_THIRD,PROFITSHARING_TO_ACCOUNT,INCOME_PROMOTION,INCOME_COOPERATION,INCOME_ASSET,INCOME_OPERATION,INCOME_IOT,REFUND_TRANSFER_TO_THIRD,REFUND_TO_ACCOUNT,REFUND_INCOME_TO_THIRD,WITHDRAW_TO_THIRD,ACCOUNT_WITHDRAW,PRESENT,PACKAGE,CPC,CPCHARGE|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;changeSubType|变动子类型,可用值:OTHER,CHARGE,REFUND,PURCHASE,OUT,IN,PROFITSHARING_TALLY,PROFITSHARING_THIRD,PROFITSHARING_TO_ACCOUNT,INCOME_PROMOTION,INCOME_COOPERATION,INCOME_ASSET,INCOME_OPERATION,INCOME_IOT,REFUND_TRANSFER_TO_THIRD,REFUND_TO_ACCOUNT,REFUND_INCOME_TO_THIRD,WITHDRAW_TO_THIRD,ACCOUNT_WITHDRAW,PRESENT,PACKAGE,CPC,CPCHARGE|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;amount|动账数额如：金额、流量|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;balance|变动后账户余额|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;nonWithdrawBalance|变动后不可提现余额(如：赠送，积分兑换的都在这里)|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;orderId|关联的订单号id|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;orderNo|关联的订单号|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;payStatus|付款状态,可用值:UNPAID,PAYING,PARTIALPAID,PAID,PAIDCANCEL,PAYFAIL,DISABLE|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;payTime|付款时间|string(date-time)||
|&emsp;&emsp;payChannel|付款渠道,可用值:WECHAT_JSAPI,WECHAT_NATIVE,WECHAT_APP,WECHAT_MICROPAY,WECHAT_MWEB,WECHAT_FACEPAY,WALLET_VIRTUAL_NW,WALLET_VIRTUAL,BANK|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;billPeriod|所属账期如26010515|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;periodEndDate|所属账期截止时间|string(date-time)||
|&emsp;&emsp;transactionId|交易号(如：微信订单号)|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;outTradeNo|外部交易号(如：微信分账单号)|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;extDetailId|扩展外部交易id(微信分账detail_id)|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;extDetailNo|扩展外部交易NO(如：微信退款out_refund_no)|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;reconciliationFlag|对账状态Y、N|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;relatedTarget|关联对象|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;relatedTargetType||ResourceTypeEnums|ResourceTypeEnums|
|&emsp;&emsp;accountSubjectId|主体id由账户类型type决定(可以是机构id、全局用户id，机构会员id)|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;accountType|账户类型,可用值:ENTERPRISE_WALLET_VIRTUAL,MEMBER_WALLET_VIRTUAL,ENTERPRISE_BANK,PERSONAL_BANK|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;currency|币种（默认CNY），挂账的币种，跟账户币种没有关系,可用值:CNY,USD,EUR,GBP,JPY,CAD,CHF,AUD,SGD,RUB,FRF,DEM,HKD,TOKEN|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;accountNo|账户号|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;description|描述|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;relatedTargetIndentify|关联对象唯一标识|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;relatedTargetName|关联对象名称|string||
|properties||array||
|conditions||array|ConditionParamsWrap|

|x-auth-token|token 值|header|true|||
|pageWrapperTransactionDetailEntity|PageWrapperTransactionDetailEntity|body|true|PageWrapperTransactionDetailEntity|PageWrapperTransactionDetailEntity|
|&emsp;&emsp;success|||false|boolean||
|&emsp;&emsp;msg|||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;errorCode|||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;rowsDetailFlag|||false|boolean||
|&emsp;&emsp;total|||false|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;rows|||false|array|TransactionDetailEntity|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_remark|备注||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_created_by|创建人||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_creation_date|创建时间||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_by|最后更新人||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_date|最后更新时间||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_enabled_flag|有效性||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;id|Id||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;workspaceId|工作空间id||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;accountId|账户id||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;manageCompanyId|管理机构(1.【应用】管理该应用下所有公司和个人的账户2.【机构】管理会员账户)3.没有时填工作空间管理方公司id||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;name|变动名称||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;inOutType|出入账类型,可用值:OTHER,CHARGE,REFUND,PURCHASE,OUT,IN,PROFITSHARING_TALLY,PROFITSHARING_THIRD,PROFITSHARING_TO_ACCOUNT,INCOME_PROMOTION,INCOME_COOPERATION,INCOME_ASSET,INCOME_OPERATION,INCOME_IOT,REFUND_TRANSFER_TO_THIRD,REFUND_TO_ACCOUNT,REFUND_INCOME_TO_THIRD,WITHDRAW_TO_THIRD,ACCOUNT_WITHDRAW,PRESENT,PACKAGE,CPC,CPCHARGE||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;changeType|变动流向类型,可用值:OTHER,CHARGE,REFUND,PURCHASE,OUT,IN,PROFITSHARING_TALLY,PROFITSHARING_THIRD,PROFITSHARING_TO_ACCOUNT,INCOME_PROMOTION,INCOME_COOPERATION,INCOME_ASSET,INCOME_OPERATION,INCOME_IOT,REFUND_TRANSFER_TO_THIRD,REFUND_TO_ACCOUNT,REFUND_INCOME_TO_THIRD,WITHDRAW_TO_THIRD,ACCOUNT_WITHDRAW,PRESENT,PACKAGE,CPC,CPCHARGE||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;changeSubType|变动子类型,可用值:OTHER,CHARGE,REFUND,PURCHASE,OUT,IN,PROFITSHARING_TALLY,PROFITSHARING_THIRD,PROFITSHARING_TO_ACCOUNT,INCOME_PROMOTION,INCOME_COOPERATION,INCOME_ASSET,INCOME_OPERATION,INCOME_IOT,REFUND_TRANSFER_TO_THIRD,REFUND_TO_ACCOUNT,REFUND_INCOME_TO_THIRD,WITHDRAW_TO_THIRD,ACCOUNT_WITHDRAW,PRESENT,PACKAGE,CPC,CPCHARGE||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;amount|动账数额如：金额、流量||false|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;balance|变动后账户余额||false|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;nonWithdrawBalance|变动后不可提现余额(如：赠送，积分兑换的都在这里)||false|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;orderId|关联的订单号id||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;orderNo|关联的订单号||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;payStatus|付款状态,可用值:UNPAID,PAYING,PARTIALPAID,PAID,PAIDCANCEL,PAYFAIL,DISABLE||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;payTime|付款时间||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;payChannel|付款渠道,可用值:WECHAT_JSAPI,WECHAT_NATIVE,WECHAT_APP,WECHAT_MICROPAY,WECHAT_MWEB,WECHAT_FACEPAY,WALLET_VIRTUAL_NW,WALLET_VIRTUAL,BANK||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;billPeriod|所属账期如26010515||false|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;periodEndDate|所属账期截止时间||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;transactionId|交易号(如：微信订单号)||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;outTradeNo|外部交易号(如：微信分账单号)||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;extDetailId|扩展外部交易id(微信分账detail_id)||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;extDetailNo|扩展外部交易NO(如：微信退款out_refund_no)||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;reconciliationFlag|对账状态Y、N||false|string||

## 设备-事件分页


**接口地址**:`/zhisuancf/manage/api/v1/devices/{id}/events/page`


**请求方式**:`POST`


**请求数据类型**:`application/x-www-form-urlencoded,application/json`


**响应数据类型**:`*/*`


**接口描述**:<p>设备-事件分页</p>



**请求示例**:


```javascript
{
  "success": true,
  "msg": "",
  "errorCode": "",
  "rowsDetailFlag": true,
  "total": 0,
  "rows": [
    {
```

## 设备doAction 


**接口地址**:`/zhisuancf/manage/api/v1/devices/{id}/doAction/{action}`


**请求方式**:`POST`


**请求数据类型**:`application/x-www-form-urlencoded,application/json`


**响应数据类型**:`*/*`


**接口描述**:<p>设备doAction mock:<a href="https://www.test.zhisuancf.cn/zhisuancf/api/v1/open/rss-static/apidoc/device-api/doAction.md">https://www.test.zhisuancf.cn/zhisuancf/api/v1/open/rss-static/apidoc/device-api/doAction.md</a></p>



**请求参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 请求类型    | 是否必须 | 数据类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | -------- | -------- | ------ |
|id|设备主键id|path|true|integer(int64)||
|action|action|path|true|string||
|x-auth-token|token 值|header|true|||


**响应状态**:


| 状态码 | 说明 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | 
|200|OK|SingleResponseObject|


**响应参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- |----- | 
|success||boolean||
|msg||string||
|errorCode||string||
|data||object||


**响应示例**:
```javascript
{
	"success": true,
	"msg": "",
	"errorCode": "",
	"data": {}
}
```

## 资产设备导入


**接口地址**:`/zhisuancf/manage/api/v1/devices/asset/import`


**请求方式**:`POST`


**请求数据类型**:`application/x-www-form-urlencoded,application/json`


**响应数据类型**:`*/*`


**接口描述**:<p>资产设备导入</p>



**请求参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 请求类型    | 是否必须 | 数据类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | -------- | -------- | ------ |
|x-auth-token|token 值|header|true|||
|file||query|false|file||
|params||query|false|||


**响应状态**:


| 状态码 | 说明 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | 
|200|OK|ListResponseDeviceEntity|


**响应参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- |----- | 
|success||boolean||
|msg||string||
|errorCode||string||
|total||integer(int64)|integer(int64)|
|data||array|DeviceEntity|
|&emsp;&emsp;_remark|备注|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;_created_by|创建人|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;_creation_date|创建时间|string(date-time)||
|&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_by|最后更新人|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_date|最后更新时间|string(date-time)||
|&emsp;&emsp;_enabled_flag|有效性|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;id|Id|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;workspaceId|工作空间id|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;accountId|账户号：挂的金融账户id|integer(int64)||

|&emsp;&emsp;name|设备名称|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;sn|Sn|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;mac|MAC地址|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;productId|产品id|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;routeDeviceId|路由设备ID|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;assetCompanyId|资产所属公司(个人资产时空)|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;assetUserId|资产管理者|integer(int64)||

## ai模型列表


**接口地址**:`/zhisuancf/manage/api/v1/ai/model/all`


**请求方式**:`GET`


**请求数据类型**:`application/x-www-form-urlencoded`


**响应数据类型**:`*/*`


**接口描述**:<p>修改时间:20191230</p>



**请求参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 请求类型    | 是否必须 | 数据类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | -------- | -------- | ------ |
|x-auth-token|token 值|header|true|||


**响应状态**:


| 状态码 | 说明 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | 
|200|OK|ListResponseNamePariVO|


**响应参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- |----- | 
|success||boolean||
|msg||string||
|errorCode||string||
|total||integer(int64)|integer(int64)|
|data||array|NamePariVO|
|&emsp;&emsp;_remark|备注|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;_created_by|创建人|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;_creation_date|创建时间|string(date-time)||
|&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_by|最后更新人|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_date|最后更新时间|string(date-time)||
|&emsp;&emsp;_enabled_flag|有效性|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;code||string||
|&emsp;&emsp;name||string||
|&emsp;&emsp;value||object||
|&emsp;&emsp;provider||string||


**响应示例**:
```javascript
{
	"success": true,
	"msg": "",
	"errorCode": "",
	"total": 0,
	"data": [
		{
			"_remark": "",
			"_created_by": 0,
			"_creation_date": "",
			"_last_updated_by": 0,
			"_last_updated_date": "",
			"_enabled_flag": "",
			"code": "",
			"name": "",
			"value": {},
			"provider": ""
		}
	]
}
```


# 开放接口


## 登录


**接口地址**:`/zhisuancf/open/api/v1/login`


**请求方式**:`POST`


**请求数据类型**:`application/x-www-form-urlencoded,application/json`


**响应数据类型**:`*/*`


**接口描述**:<p>登录mock:<a href="https://www.test.zhisuancf.cn/zhisuancf/api/v1/open/rss-static/apidoc/open-api/login.md">https://www.test.zhisuancf.cn/zhisuancf/api/v1/open/rss-static/apidoc/open-api/login.md</a></p>



**请求示例**:


```javascript
{
  "loginType": "",
  "account": "",
  "email": "",
  "mobile": "",
  "password": "",
  "source": "",
  "uuid": "",
  "code": "",
  "random": "",
  "sessionTime": 0
}
```


**请求参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 请求类型    | 是否必须 | 数据类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | -------- | -------- | ------ |
|loginVo|登录参数|body|true|LoginVo|LoginVo|
|&emsp;&emsp;loginType|登录方式,可用值:account_password:账号+密码,email_password:邮箱+密码,mobile_password:手机号+密码,mobile_smscode_password:手机号+密码+短信验证码,mobile_smscode:手机号+短信验证码,third_account:第三方账户登录||true|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;account|用户账号||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;email|邮箱||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;mobile|用户手机号||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;password|用户账号密码||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;source|第三方source||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;uuid|第三方uuid||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;code|校验码||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;random|随机数||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;sessionTime|会话时长秒||false|integer(int64)||
|x-app-key|appKey |header|false|string||


**响应状态**:


| 状态码 | 说明 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | 
|200|OK|SingleResponseUserDTO|


**响应参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- |----- | 
|success||boolean||
|msg||string||
|errorCode||string||
|data||UserDTO|UserDTO|
|&emsp;&emsp;_remark|备注|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;_created_by|创建人|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;_creation_date|创建时间|string(date-time)||
|&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_by|最后更新人|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_date|最后更新时间|string(date-time)||
|&emsp;&emsp;_enabled_flag|有效性|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;id||integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;uuid|UUID|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;mobile|手机号|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;email|邮箱|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;account|用户账号|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;realName|真实姓名|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;idCard|身份证号|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;sex|性别SexTypeEnums,可用值:MALE:男,FEMALE:女,UNKNOWN:保密|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;intro|简介|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;nickName|用户昵称|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;manageStatus|管理状态参考：ManageStatusEnums,可用值:DISABLE:禁用,ENABLE:启用,ENABLE_AND_INACTIVE:启用&待激活|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;imei|IMEI|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;registerTime|注册时间|string(date-time)||
|&emsp;&emsp;registerChannel|注册渠道|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;lastMdfPwdTime|最后一次修改密码时间|string(date-time)||

|&emsp;&emsp;workspaceId|工作空间id|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;appKey|appKey|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;appName|appName|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;workspaceGroupId|工作空间对应的组id|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;workspaceGroupType|工作空间对应的组类型,可用值:OTHER:其他,PLATFORM:平台,WORKSPACE:工作空间,SYS_USER:系统用户组,PROJECT:项目组,BRAND:品牌（工作空间级）,AGENCY:代理,SOLUTION:方案,MANUFACTURE:制造厂,SALE:销售组,FAE:FAE组,DISTRIBUTOR:分销商,BRAND_DISTRIBUT:品牌分销,AGENCY_DISTRIBUT:代理分销,PARTNER_GROUP:合作伙伴组,COMPUTILITY_CENTER:算力中心,CONTRACT:合同,ENTERPRISE_TRAFFIC_ACCOUNT:企业(机构)流量账户,MEMBER_TRAFFIC_ACCOUNT:企业(机构)会员(用户在机构下的)流量账户,DEPARTMENT:部门|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;workspaceCompanyId|工作空间对应的公司id|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;companyId|当前登录人员归属公司id,任何人只能挂一个公司下面|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;companyIdentify|归属公司identify|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;companyRoles|企业角色|array|string|

|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;groupType|组类型（冗余同group对应的组类型）,可用值:OTHER:其他,PLATFORM:平台,WORKSPACE:工作空间,SYS_USER:系统用户组,PROJECT:项目组,BRAND:品牌（工作空间级）,AGENCY:代理,SOLUTION:方案,MANUFACTURE:制造厂,SALE:销售组,FAE:FAE组,DISTRIBUTOR:分销商,BRAND_DISTRIBUT:品牌分销,AGENCY_DISTRIBUT:代理分销,PARTNER_GROUP:合作伙伴组,COMPUTILITY_CENTER:算力中心,CONTRACT:合同,ENTERPRISE_TRAFFIC_ACCOUNT:企业(机构)流量账户,MEMBER_TRAFFIC_ACCOUNT:企业(机构)会员(用户在机构下的)流量账户,DEPARTMENT:部门|string||

## 企业登录(clientId登录)


**接口地址**:`/zhisuancf/open/api/v1/enterprise/accessToken`


**请求方式**:`POST`


**请求数据类型**:`application/x-www-form-urlencoded,application/json`


**响应数据类型**:`*/*`


**接口描述**:<p>用于系统对接api调用</p>



**请求示例**:


```javascript
{
  "clientId": "",
  "clientSecret": "",
  "sessionTime": 0
}
```


**请求参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 请求类型    | 是否必须 | 数据类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | -------- | -------- | ------ |
|clientLoginVo|登录参数|body|true|ClientLoginVo|ClientLoginVo|
|&emsp;&emsp;clientId|客户id（即账号）||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;clientSecret|密钥||false|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;sessionTime|会话时长秒||false|integer(int64)||
|x-app-key|appKey |header|false|string||


**响应状态**:


| 状态码 | 说明 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | 
|200|OK|SingleResponseAccessTokenVO|


**响应参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- |----- | 
|success||boolean||
|msg||string||
|errorCode||string||
|data||AccessTokenVO|AccessTokenVO|
|&emsp;&emsp;accessToken|accessToken|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;sessionTime|会话有效时长秒|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;info||UserDTO|UserDTO|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_remark|备注|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_created_by|创建人|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_creation_date|创建时间|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_by|最后更新人|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_date|最后更新时间|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_enabled_flag|有效性|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;id||integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;uuid|UUID|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;mobile|手机号|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;email|邮箱|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;account|用户账号|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;realName|真实姓名|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;idCard|身份证号|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;sex|性别SexTypeEnums,可用值:MALE:男,FEMALE:女,UNKNOWN:保密|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;intro|简介|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;nickName|用户昵称|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;manageStatus|管理状态参考：ManageStatusEnums,可用值:DISABLE:禁用,ENABLE:启用,ENABLE_AND_INACTIVE:启用&待激活|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;imei|IMEI|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;registerTime|注册时间|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;registerChannel|注册渠道|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;lastMdfPwdTime|最后一次修改密码时间|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;cancellationTime|注销时间|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;contactWay|联系方式|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;lastLoginTime|最后一次登录时间|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;workspaceId|工作空间id|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;appKey|appKey|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;appName|appName|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;workspaceGroupId|工作空间对应的组id|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;workspaceGroupType|工作空间对应的组类型,可用值:OTHER:其他,PLATFORM:平台,WORKSPACE:工作空间,SYS_USER:系统用户组,PROJECT:项目组,BRAND:品牌（工作空间级）,AGENCY:代理,SOLUTION:方案,MANUFACTURE:制造厂,SALE:销售组,FAE:FAE组,DISTRIBUTOR:分销商,BRAND_DISTRIBUT:品牌分销,AGENCY_DISTRIBUT:代理分销,PARTNER_GROUP:合作伙伴组,COMPUTILITY_CENTER:算力中心,CONTRACT:合同,ENTERPRISE_TRAFFIC_ACCOUNT:企业(机构)流量账户,MEMBER_TRAFFIC_ACCOUNT:企业(机构)会员(用户在机构下的)流量账户,DEPARTMENT:部门|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;workspaceCompanyId|工作空间对应的公司id|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;companyId|当前登录人员归属公司id,任何人只能挂一个公司下面|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;companyIdentify|归属公司identify|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;companyRoles|企业角色|array|string|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;companyName|当前登录人员归属公司,任何人只能挂一个公司下面|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;positions|当前登录人员职位|array|string|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;accessToken|accessToken|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;workspaces|工作空间列表|array|ManageWorkspaceDTO|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_remark|备注|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_created_by|创建人|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_creation_date|创建时间|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_by|最后更新人|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_last_updated_date|最后更新时间|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;_enabled_flag|有效性|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;id|Id|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;name|名称|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;code|编码|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;manageStatus|管理状态参考：ManageStatusEnums,可用值:DISABLE:禁用,ENABLE:启用,ENABLE_AND_INACTIVE:启用&待激活|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;parentId|父ID|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;tag|标签|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;companyId|公司(企业)id|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;description|描述|string||

# 登录授权


## 登陆授权-密码|密钥-修改


**接口地址**:`/zhisuancf/manage/api/v1/password/modify`


**请求方式**:`POST`


**请求数据类型**:`application/x-www-form-urlencoded,application/json`


**响应数据类型**:`*/*`


**接口描述**:<p>密码|密钥-修改</p>



**请求示例**:


```javascript
{
  "workspaceId": 0,
  "appId": 0,
  "password": "",
  "newPassword": "",
  "newConfirmPassword": ""
}
```


**请求参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 请求类型    | 是否必须 | 数据类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | -------- | -------- | ------ |
|x-auth-token|token 值|header|true|||
|重置密码/密钥|重置密码/密钥|body|true|重置密码/密钥|重置密码/密钥|
|&emsp;&emsp;workspaceId|工作空间id||false|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;appId|应用ID||false|integer(int64)||
|&emsp;&emsp;password|旧密码||true|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;newPassword|新密码||true|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;newConfirmPassword|新确认密码||true|string||


**响应状态**:


| 状态码 | 说明 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | 
|200|OK|BaseResponse|


**响应参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- |----- | 
|success||boolean||
|msg||string||
|errorCode||string||


**响应示例**:
```javascript
{
	"success": true,
	"msg": "",
	"errorCode": ""
}
```

|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;agencyId|代理商id||false|integer||

|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;mode|产品模式:代理、分销,可用值:OTHER:其他,PLATFORM:平台,WORKSPACE:工作空间,SYS_USER:系统用户组,PROJECT:项目组,BRAND:品牌（工作空间级）,AGENCY:代理,SOLUTION:方案,MANUFACTURE:制造厂,SALE:销售组,FAE:FAE组,DISTRIBUTOR:分销商,BRAND_DISTRIBUT:品牌分销,AGENCY_DISTRIBUT:代理分销,PARTNER_GROUP:合作伙伴组,COMPUTILITY_CENTER:算力中心,CONTRACT:合同,ENTERPRISE_TRAFFIC_ACCOUNT:企业(机构)流量账户,MEMBER_TRAFFIC_ACCOUNT:企业(机构)会员(用户在机构下的)流量账户,DEPARTMENT:部门|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;companyId|代理商/分销商公司id|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;parentCompanyId|上级代理公司id|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;productId|产品id|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;name|代理产品名称|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;model|型号|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;status|产品状态ProductStatusEnums,可用值:DEVELOPING,WAITING_CHECK,CHECKING,CHECK_UNPASS,CHECK_PASS,WAITING_SHELF,ON_SHELF,OFF_SHELF,HALT|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;manufacturerId|制造厂商，生产厂商。是指有设备有场地制造产品的厂家|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;tag|标签|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;originalPrice|产品原价(元)|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;price|产品市场价格(元)|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;maturityDate|到期日期时间|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;parentId|父id|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;categoryDictIds|产品分类|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;saleAreaIds|可售区域|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;saleStartTime|可售开始时间|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;saleEndTime|可售结束时间|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;tagArr|产品标签|array|string|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;companyName|代理商公司id|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;manufacturerName|设备厂商|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;creator|建档人名称|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;lastUpdator|最后建档更新人名称|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;parentCategoryName|上级分类id|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;categoryName|分类id|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;attachmentInfo||ProductAttachInfoVO|ProductAttachInfoVO|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;features||ProductFeaturesInfoVO|ProductFeaturesInfoVO|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;categoryIds|产品分类字典id列表|array|integer|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;cf|产品消费特性参与数据字典：消费级PROD-CG，PROD-ENT企业级|array|string|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;unit|规格单位(支，箱，个，盘, G,组,件),可用值:NONE:无,PCS:个/件/台,NUMBER_OF_TIMES:次数,GB:gb|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;bd|计费维度,可用值:NONE:无,BQ:按量如：次、按token,PQ:包量:月/季度/年 ,RS:资源型：个，件箱,ET:事件型|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;cycleUnit|计费周期单位,可用值:NONE:无,NANOSECONDS:纳秒,MICROSECONDS:微秒,MILLISECONDS:毫秒,SECONDS:秒,MINUTES:分钟,HOURS:小时,DAYS:天,WEEK:周,MONTH:月,QUARTER:季度,YEAR:年|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;cycle|周期|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;mf|厂商|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;serviceInfo||ProductSeviceInfoVO|ProductSeviceInfoVO|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;cycle|周期|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;cycleUnit|周期单位,可用值:NONE:无,NANOSECONDS:纳秒,MICROSECONDS:微秒,MILLISECONDS:毫秒,SECONDS:秒,MINUTES:分钟,HOURS:小时,DAYS:天,WEEK:周,MONTH:月,QUARTER:季度,YEAR:年|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;cycleOverdueFee|周期超期费用|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;packageInfo||ProductPackageInfoVO|ProductPackageInfoVO|
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;contractCycle|合约周期，如一年合约|integer||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;contractCycleUnit|合约周期单位,可用值:NONE:无,NANOSECONDS:纳秒,MICROSECONDS:微秒,MILLISECONDS:毫秒,SECONDS:秒,MINUTES:分钟,HOURS:小时,DAYS:天,WEEK:周,MONTH:月,QUARTER:季度,YEAR:年|string||
|&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;&emsp;autoRenewal|自动续签|boolean||

## 订单-详情


**接口地址**:`/zhisuancf/manage/api/v1/orders/{id}`


**请求方式**:`GET`


**请求数据类型**:`application/x-www-form-urlencoded`


**响应数据类型**:`*/*`


**接口描述**:<p>订单-详情</p>



**请求参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 请求类型    | 是否必须 | 数据类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | -------- | -------- | ------ |
|id||path|true|integer(int64)||
|x-auth-token|token 值|header|true|||


**响应状态**:


| 状态码 | 说明 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- | 
|200|OK|SingleResponseOrderDTO|


**响应参数**:


| 参数名称 | 参数说明 | 类型 | schema |
| -------- | -------- | ----- |----- | 
|success||boolean||
|msg||string||
|errorCode||string||

|&emsp;&emsp;amountPaid|订单已付金额总金额(如充100送20时此处填100。充100送20时用了【10元券或其他金额抵】扣时此处填90并另记一笔(如果有多种类型组合则记多笔)【10元券或其他金额】扣时的账单明细，订单超时时【10元券或其他金额】应该退回)|number||
|&emsp;&emsp;payChannel|付款渠道,可用值:WECHAT_JSAPI,WECHAT_NATIVE,WECHAT_APP,WECHAT_MICROPAY,WECHAT_MWEB,WECHAT_FACEPAY,WALLET_VIRTUAL_NW,WALLET_VIRTUAL,BANK|string||
